# The Tide Mother — Image Prompts for Grok Imagine (cinematic blocking)

*171 beats, 31:59 total. Every beat with a conversation or a face-off is shot **from behind the listener**: the listener's back is in the foreground and the speaker faces the camera, so the two sides always face each other. When both sides speak, the beat has an **A/B pair of shots** (shot and reverse shot). Nights are truly dark, lit only by four fixed medieval light sources. Visuals follow the original FABLEON Visual Style Profile unchanged.*


---

## PROLOGUE — THE LAW OF KELVHAVEN


### Prologue — Kelvhaven


**Beat 001 · 0:00–0:06 · 6s · Opening hook**

- **Script segment:** "A storm-black sea. Somewhere beneath it, something opens its eye."
- **Season:** Start of winter (the great storm): cold rain, wind and sea spray; no snow
- **Location sheet:** none (open sea or underwater)
- **Image prompt:** Photorealistic cinematic film still, dark fantasy, shot on 35mm, fine film grain, high-fidelity textures, shallow depth of field, natural bokeh, desaturated high-contrast color grade, 16:9. Extreme close-up, underwater: a single huge luminous cyan slit-pupil eye opening in pitch-black water, faint bioluminescent blue veins around it. Setting: deep underwater in a cold northern sea, drifting particles, kelp shadows, light fading from the surface far above. Lighting: darkness pierced by electric-blue bioluminescent glow. Mood: ominous, mysterious. Palette: storm slate grey, fog grey, driftwood brown and wet-wool grey. No text, no watermark, no modern objects.
- **Camera angle:** Extreme close-up, underwater
- **Lighting:** darkness pierced by electric-blue bioluminescent glow
- **Mood:** ominous, mysterious

**Beat 003 · 0:21–0:36 · 15s · Dialogue and character emotion**
